id: GH-86
title: 'Onboarding: a step-by-step setup after creating a business'
type: feature
status: developing
current_role: 'PM: final validation'
flags:
  touches_ui: true
  touches_copy: true
returns:
  Tech Lead→Developer:extract-integrations: 1
  Tech Lead→Developer:extract-stock-entry: 1
  Tech Lead→Developer:wiring: 2
return_history:
- pair: Tech Lead→Developer:extract-integrations
  category: spec-missing-test
  reason: The brief's "Buscar mi chat marks the form dirty" test types a token first,
    so the form is already dirty before the lookup and the test passes whatever the
    lookup does; make the lookup the only change.
- pair: Tech Lead→Developer:extract-stock-entry
  category: process-suite-red
  reason: 'The head''s declared web-e2e check is red. In my combined run, catalog.spec
    timed out waiting for EntradaStock''s Gramos field. The Developer must get web-e2e
    green on this head, or show that the base commit fails the same way. The refactor
    itself checked out: no existing test edited, the new seam test exists, invariants
    1–10 are untouched.'
- pair: Tech Lead→Developer:wiring
  category: spec-scope
  reason: 'The brief''s Design item was not done: compare.sh was never run for the
    seven new Onboarding screens (OnboardingVariedad … OnboardingListo). §10 requires
    a screen to be checked against its comparison before it is delivered. The step
    wiring, the step tests and the e2e runs otherwise match the brief.'
- pair: Tech Lead→Developer:wiring
  category: standard-other
  reason: The OnboardingListo entry in screens.json now points at /onboarding/done
    with no steps. That path sends anyone without the listo flag to Inicio, so the
    entry compares Inicio against the Listo design and the Listo screen has still
    not been compared.
escalated: null
escalation_class: null
questions_for_human: []
proposed_invariants:
- Every Onboarding step calls its owner's existing use case and returns its refusal
  codes, with no rule restated in the onboarding hexagon.
- The Onboarding state table carries `business_id`, follows the naming convention,
  has a declared label for ADR-0015 and is never counted by any owner's `has_records()`.
- A use case that changes Onboarding state commits once through the `Transaction`
  port, together with the owner's write when it is a step.
- The business is always read from `CurrentBusinessId`, never from a request body
  or path parameter, in every Onboarding route.
- A use case that sets `ended_at` re-reads both required facts through the owners'
  ports in the same transaction, and stores nothing when either is unmet.
- '`ended_at` once set is never cleared or moved by any route, use case or migration.'
- No `GET` handler in the onboarding hexagon writes to the database.
- A front step screen reuses its owner's form component and its owner's route, and
  holds no validation of its own.
- 'The progress answer is total: `resume` is empty only when `ended_at` is set, and
  every other state names a step or `finish` for the front to draw.'
- 'The Onboarding keeps no state in browser storage or client memory beyond the current
  history entry: the variety a step targets always comes from `/onboarding-progress`.'
- An extraction subtask changes no behaviour of the owner screen it touches, and edits
  no existing test.
- 'The front draws "Listo" only from an `over: true` answer, never from a state where
  `ended_at` is null.'
- The Inicio notice for the `finish` state sends `advance` on tap and writes nothing
  on render.
discard_reason: null
branch: agentic/GH-86
pull_request: null
subtasks:
  design:
    id: design
    title: Design the Onboarding steps, "Listo" and Inicio's "Seguí con la configuración"
      notice from tokens
    brief: 'Draw the Onboarding''s screens in `docs/product/design-system/pantallas/`,
      following onboarding.md:

      - the step layout: one centred phone-width column, with the form-or-sheet chrome;

      - the progress indicator: "Paso N de 6" in words as well as shape, never by
      colour alone;

      - the six steps;

      - "Listo";

      - the Inicio notice "Seguí con la configuración" ("N de 6 pasos" and "Falta:
      …", or "6 de 6 pasos" with no Falta line), on `Inicio`, `InicioOscuro` and `InicioCompu`.


      **Constraints:**

      - tokens and existing components only;

      - no new colours, illustrations or animation;

      - both themes;

      - nothing clipped at 320 to 430 px or at 130 % text;

      - controls at least 44 px tall, inputs at least 16 px.


      **Copy:** all in Rioplatense Spanish with vos, from onboarding.md:

      - every step''s title and its one-line why;

      - the buttons and links: "Atrás", "Saltar", "Saltar el resto", "Cerrar", "Dejar
      así", "Seguir", "Agregar otra variedad";

      - "Enviale el link a tu socio";

      - "Cuando quieras, publicalo desde Variedades → Mandar catálogo";

      - step 2''s sentence that with no cost the lot costs $0;

      - "Listo"''s lines, "Cargar mi primera venta" and "Ir a Inicio".


      **Registry:** register each screen in `scripts/design/screens.json` as a form
      or sheet, under:

      - `/onboarding/variety`

      - `/onboarding/stock`

      - `/onboarding/catalog-message`

      - `/onboarding/bot`

      - `/onboarding/partner`

      - `/onboarding/categories`

      - `/onboarding/done`


      **Acceptance:**

      - every screen renders through `compare.sh` in both themes, phone and computer;

      - `tests/test_design_contrast.py` passes.'
    seams:
    - scripts/design/screens.json entries
    - tests/test_design_contrast.py
    depends_on: []
    status: approved
    screens:
    - OnboardingVariedad
    - OnboardingStock
    - OnboardingMensaje
    - OnboardingBot
    - OnboardingSocio
    - OnboardingCategorias
    - OnboardingListo
    - Inicio
    - InicioCompu
    - InicioOscuro
    branch: agentic/GH-86.design
    base: 07d1f53
    reviewed_head: 38210d8c83d87c81bcc1bdf37c521b75fe26fc6e
  state:
    id: state
    title: 'The onboarding hexagon''s state: table, migration 0013, seeding on a new
      business, removal and isolation coverage, ADR-0032'
    brief: '**Hexagon.** Create the `app/onboarding/` skeleton (`domain/`, `application/`,
      `adapters/`):

      - add it to `HEXAGON_ROOTS`;

      - add its tables module to `TABLE_MODULES` and `app/tables.py`.


      **Domain.** An `Onboarding` entity holds:

      - passes for `header_footer`, `bot`, `partner` and `categories`, each None,
      `DONE` or `SKIPPED` (an enum);

      - `ended_at: datetime | None`.


      `end(at)` sets `ended_at` only while it is None, and never moves it.


      **Table.** `onboarding`, in `app/onboarding/adapters/tables.py`, created by
      migration `0013_onboarding.py` (revises 0012):

      - `id`;

      - `business_id`, not null, FK to `business`, with `uq_onboarding_business_id`;

      - `header_footer_pass`, `bot_pass`, `partner_pass` and `categories_pass`, nullable
      strings, each with a check constraint allowing `done` or `skipped`;

      - `ended_at`, a nullable `UtcDateTime`;

      - `created_at`, a not-null `UtcDateTime`.


      Names come from `NAMING_CONVENTION`, with `op.f(...)` in the migration (§11).
      There is no backfill.


      **Repository.** An `Onboardings` port and `SqlOnboardings`, built for one business:

      - every statement is filtered on `business_id`;

      - it flushes and never commits.


      **Seeding.** `app/onboarding/adapters/opening.py`''s `OnboardingOpening.opened(business_id)`
      inserts the row. It is added to `CompositeOpening` in `app/main.py`.


      **Removal.** `onboarding` goes in `LABEL_AS_BUSINESS`, exported (not withheld).


      **Isolation.** Seed one row in `tests/test_business_isolation.py`.


      **ADR-0032**, "The Onboarding stores only its passes and its end; done-ness
      is derived, steps write through their owners, and its API is `/onboarding-progress`".
      It states:

      1. the row marks who gets it, with no backfill;

      2. required steps are derived and never latched, and `progress()` is total,
      including its `finish` state;

      3. `ended_at` is set only by `advance` (both required facts re-read in its transaction)
      or by the import, only while null, and never cleared;

      4. steps write through their owners, and targets come from the progress answer;

      5. the import ends the Onboarding through a port it declares, in its single
      commit;

      6. the front owns the `onboarding` segment and the API takes `onboarding-progress`
      (ADR-0019), leaving ADR-0024 and ADR-0030 untouched;

      7. "Listo" is drawn only from an `over: true` write answer.


      **CONTEXT.md:** check the Onboarding entry. Adjust it only if it contradicts.


      **Tests:**

      - domain unit tests for `end`;

      - accepting a new-business invitation leaves exactly one row, and accepting
      a join invitation adds none;

      - ownership, naming (no allowlist change) and schema tests;

      - removal: 0 rows remain, exported count = deleted count, and the dry run counts
      it.'
    seams:
    - Onboarding entity (domain)
    - POST /invitations/{token}/accept
    - business removal command (remove_business use case / CLI)
    - tests/migrations schema checks
    depends_on: []
    status: approved
    branch: agentic/GH-86.state
    base: 07d1f53
    reviewed_head: 609f3f47473e55e21eab1a19f3e3c880961f7071
  progress-domain:
    id: progress-domain
    title: progress() and the advance, pass and skip-rest use cases, over fake fact
      ports
    brief: "**`progress()`.** A pure, total `progress(facts, onboarding) -> Progress`\
      \ in `app/onboarding/domain/progress.py`. Its facts are:\n- the varieties as\
      \ `(id, name, is_priced_today)`, by id;\n- the variety ids with a stock entry,\
      \ and the entry count;\n- whether the bot is saved;\n- the user count.\n\nIt\
      \ answers:\n- `over`: `ended_at is not None`.\n- `steps`, one per step 1 to\
      \ 6, each `done`, `skipped` or `pending`:\n  - step 1 is done when some variety\
      \ is priced today;\n  - step 2 is done when an entry exists;\n  - steps 3 and\
      \ 6 follow their pass only;\n  - step 4 is done when the bot is saved or its\
      \ pass is `done`;\n  - step 5 is done when there are 2 or more users or its\
      \ pass is `done`.\n- `count`: the steps done or skipped.\n- `resume`: the first\
      \ pending step. With none pending, it is `finish` while `ended_at` is None,\
      \ and None only when over.\n- `can_skip_rest`.\n- `varieties_count` and `stock_entries_count`.\n\
      - `price_target`: the lowest-id unpriced variety.\n- `stock_target`: the lowest-id\
      \ variety priced today with no entry.\n\n**Use cases** in `application/`, each\
      \ committing once through `Transaction`:\n- **`read_progress`:** never writes;\
      \ with no row it answers not offered.\n- **`advance`:** reads the facts and\
      \ the row in one session. It ends the Onboarding only when `ended_at` is None,\
      \ every optional step is done or skipped, and both required steps are done.\n\
      - **`record_pass(step, as_)`:**\n  - only optional steps with `as_` set to `done`\
      \ or `skipped`; anything else raises `StepInvalid` (`onboarding.step_invalid`);\n\
      \  - on an Onboarding already over, it stores nothing;\n  - otherwise it sets\
      \ the pass and runs the ending check, in one commit.\n- **`skip_rest`:**\n \
      \ - raises `RequiredStepsUnmet` (`onboarding.required_steps_unmet`) unless both\
      \ required steps are done;\n  - otherwise it sets `skipped` on every optional\
      \ step that is neither done nor passed, and ends the Onboarding.\n- **No row:**\
      \ every write raises `OnboardingNotFound` (`onboarding.not_found`, a LookupError,\
      \ kind `gone`).\n\n**Ports**, declared in `application/ports.py`:\n- `VarietyFacts`;\n\
      - `StockFacts`;\n- `BotFacts`;\n- `UserFacts`.\n\n**Tests:**\n- a pure table\
      \ test of `progress()`: every step state, the count, resume order, `finish`,\
      \ the targets, and resume never None while `ended_at` is None;\n- use-case tests\
      \ over fakes:\n  - a pass ends the Onboarding;\n  - a pass is recorded with\
      \ `over: false` when step 1 is unmet;\n  - `advance` ends it after the price\
      \ returns;\n  - `skip_rest` is refused or accepted;\n  - a write on an Onboarding\
      \ already over stores nothing;\n  - `end` never moves `ended_at`;\n  - an invalid\
      \ step is refused;\n  - a write with no row raises `OnboardingNotFound`."
    seams:
    - progress() (domain function)
    - read_progress, advance, record_pass, skip_rest use cases over fake ports
    depends_on:
    - state
    status: approved
    branch: agentic/GH-86.progress-domain
    base: 0623eb5
    reviewed_head: d04e79f62d4ac82f3299b0370520551ca5a787fd
  progress-http:
    id: progress-http
    title: Fact adapters in the owners and the /onboarding-progress routes
    brief: "**Fact adapters.** Each is built for one business:\n- `app/varieties/adapters/onboarding_facts.py`:\
      \ each variety's id and name, and whether it has a current price today, by id.\
      \ It reuses the varieties repository's list with today's price.\n- `app/stock/adapters/onboarding_facts.py`:\
      \ the distinct variety ids with an entry, and the entry count.\n- `app/pinned_catalog/adapters/onboarding_facts.py`:\
      \ whether bot settings are saved. It reads presence only, never the token.\n\
      - `app/accounts/adapters/onboarding_facts.py`: the user count, filtered by `business_id`.\n\
      \nWire them as `(Session, business_id)` factories in `app/main.py`.\n\n**Router.**\
      \ `app/onboarding/adapters/router.py`, included with `require_session`, with\
      \ the business from `CurrentBusinessId`:\n- `GET /onboarding-progress`;\n- `POST\
      \ /onboarding-progress/passes` with the bare body `{step: str, as: str}`;\n\
      - `POST /onboarding-progress/skip-rest`;\n- `POST /onboarding-progress/advance`.\n\
      \nEvery route answers:\n\n`{offered, over, count, resume, steps: [{step, state}],\
      \ can_skip_rest, varieties_count, stock_entries_count, price_target, stock_target}`\n\
      \n**Contract.** Add the three codes to `web/src/api/contract.json`, and add\
      \ `onboarding` to `frontSegments`.\n\n**HTTP tests** (`client` and `other_client`,\
      \ the movable clock):\n- A new business answers `offered`, count 0, resume step\
      \ 1.\n- A price effective tomorrow does not make step 1 done.\n- With the bot\
      \ saved through Probarlo and a second user, but no pass: count 4, resume `header_footer`.\
      \ Then the passes for steps 3 and 6 end it.\n- With every optional step passed\
      \ and then the only price deleted:\n  - the last pass answers `over: false`;\n\
      \  - after the price is re-added through `/varieties/{id}/prices`, `GET` answers\
      \ resume `finish`;\n  - then `advance` answers `over: true`.\n- An unpriced\
      \ variety is the `price_target`. A price-only submit leaves exactly one variety,\
      \ and that variety becomes the `stock_target`.\n- A partner session in the same\
      \ business sees the same targets.\n- `GET` issues no write.\n- With no row,\
      \ `GET` answers `offered: false` and every write answers 404.\n\n**Coverage:**\n\
      - `tests/test_gated_routes.py`;\n- the isolation sweep, with `BODIES`;\n- `tests/test_refusal_codes.py`;\n\
      - a browser navigation to `/onboarding/variety` gets the shell, and `/onboarding-progress`\
      \ requested as JSON reaches the API."
    seams:
    - GET /onboarding-progress
    - POST /onboarding-progress/passes
    - POST /onboarding-progress/skip-rest
    - POST /onboarding-progress/advance
    depends_on:
    - progress-domain
    status: approved
    branch: agentic/GH-86.progress-http
    base: 9c1d010
    reviewed_head: 2d774063a1b309372951f90859f53fd8327176ec
  import-ending:
    id: import-ending
    title: A successful spreadsheet import ends the Onboarding in its single commit
    brief: '**Port.** In `app/spreadsheet_import/application/ports.py`, declare `OnboardingEnding.imported(at,
      transaction)`.


      **Implementation.** `app/onboarding/adapters/import_port.py`:

      - sets `ended_at` through `Onboarding.end` when the business has a row and `ended_at`
      is None;

      - does nothing when there is no row;

      - never commits.


      **Write path.** `write_import` takes it as a keyword argument and calls it after
      the last writer, under the same `NoCommit`. The CLI builds it and commits once.


      **Untouched:** eligibility; the dry run never calls it.


      **Tests:**

      - with passes recorded and no owner records, the import is accepted and `ended_at`
      is set;

      - with a step-1 variety saved, the import is refused with `import.business_has_records`
      and the row is unchanged;

      - a dry run, and a refused real run, both leave `ended_at` null;

      - a business with no row imports as before.'
    seams:
    - write_import use case
    - scripts/import-spreadsheet.sh CLI (adapters/cli.py main)
    depends_on:
    - state
    status: approved
    branch: agentic/GH-86.import-ending
    base: 0623eb5
    reviewed_head: 695e4bfe2984d13f99904b11614a987f7ef66293

  shell:
    id: shell
    title: 'Onboarding shell: routes, step frame, progress client, landing after a
      new-business accept, Listo and Inicio''s notice'
    brief: 'Build the shell from the design. Each step body is an empty slot behind
      a step registry.


      **Client** (`web/src/api/onboarding.ts`):

      - `GET /onboarding-progress` and the three writes;

      - keys in `keys.ts`;

      - frozen queries.


      **Routes:**

      - `/onboarding` resolves to the resume step;

      - the six step paths;

      - `/onboarding/done`.


      All of them use chrome `none`.


      **Leaving for Inicio.** Any Onboarding path goes to Inicio when the answer says
      `offered: false` or `over: true`. The one exception is `/onboarding/done` entered
      with the history state `{listo: true}`. Without that flag, `/onboarding/done`
      goes to Inicio.


      **The step frame** (`web/src/screens/onboarding/OnboardingStep.tsx`):

      - the title, the one-line why and "Paso N de 6" with the indicator;

      - "Atrás";

      - "Saltar" on optional steps, which sends a `skipped` pass;

      - "Saltar el resto" only when `can_skip_rest`, which sends `skip-rest`;

      - "Cerrar" to Inicio;

      - one leave guard, fed by the slot''s `onDirtyChange`.


      **`afterProgress(answer)`:**

      - `over: true`: open `/onboarding/done` with `{listo: true}`;

      - otherwise: open the resume step.


      **Listo** (`web/src/screens/onboarding/Listo.tsx`):

      - one line per step: "Hecho", "Hecho · N variedades", or "Para después — en
      Configuración → <grupo>" / "en Variedades";

      - "Cargar mi primera venta" opens Nueva venta, which returns to Inicio;

      - "Ir a Inicio".


      **Landing.** After a `new_business` accept (`session.ts`, `Invitacion.tsx`),
      go to `/onboarding`. A `join_business` accept goes to Inicio.


      **Inicio''s notices.** The pure `inicioNotices({ onboarding, pricedToday, pinOutOfDate
      })` lives in `web/src/screens/inicioNotices.ts`. It follows results.md''s order:

      1. slot 1: "Seguí con la configuración" while the Onboarding is offered and
      not over, otherwise "Arrancá por las variedades";

      2. slot 2: the pin notice.


      Slot 1 stays empty until both answers have arrived. On a failure, the existing
      "Sin conexión" or the generic retry shows.


      **The notice itself:**

      - It reads "N de 6 pasos" and "Falta: …", or "6 de 6 pasos" with no Falta line
      in `finish`.

      - It cannot be dismissed.

      - A tap opens the resume step.

      - In `finish`, a tap sends `advance` and then calls `afterProgress`.

      - A render never writes.


      **Docs.** Update accounts.md''s landing line.


      **Tests:**

      - a table test of `inicioNotices`;

      - Inicio with answers arriving in either order never shows "Arrancá" for an
      Onboarding business;

      - the `finish` notice sends `advance` only on a tap;

      - "Listo" opens only after `over: true`;

      - a deep link to "Listo" without the flag goes to Inicio;

      - an Onboarding path of a business that is over, or never offered it, goes to
      Inicio;

      - the accept landings;

      - the frame''s chrome and leave confirmation;

      - "Saltar el resto" is hidden until both required steps are done;

      - Nueva venta opened from "Listo" returns to Inicio;

      - nothing in the console or storage;

      - `compare.sh` for Inicio and InicioCompu.'
    seams:
    - inicioNotices (pure function)
    - Inicio screen (Testing Library, fetch faked)
    - Onboarding routes and step frame (Testing Library)
    - Listo screen (Testing Library)
    - Invitacion accept flow (Testing Library)
    depends_on:
    - design
    - progress-http
    status: approved
    screens:
    - Inicio
    - InicioCompu
    - InicioOscuro
    - OnboardingListo
    branch: agentic/GH-86.shell
    base: 612131c
    reviewed_head: 704298d96486df87853b641700a23611ba9fbff4
  wiring:
    id: wiring
    title: Wire the six Onboarding steps to the extracted owner forms, and the end-to-end
      run
    brief: 'Fill the step slots. Each step saves through its owner''s route first,
      then sends the onboarding write, then calls `afterProgress`.


      **Step 1:**

      - With a `price_target`: price-only `PriceForm` for that variety, effective
      today.

      - Without one: `VarietyNameForm`, then `PriceForm`.

      - A refused price keeps what was typed, and a retry sends only the price.

      - Afterwards, re-read `GET` and go to the resume step.


      **Step 2:**

      - `StockEntryForm`, fixed to `stock_target` and to `opening_stock`, dated today.

      - It says that with no cost the lot costs $0.

      - Then "Agregar otra variedad" or "Seguir"; "Seguir" sends `advance`.


      **Step 3:** `CatalogMessageForm`.

      - "Guardar" saves, then sends pass `done`.

      - "Dejar así" sends pass `done`.

      - "Saltar" sends `skipped`.


      **Step 4:** `IntegrationsForm` without the catalog link, followed by the line
      "Cuando quieras, publicalo desde Variedades → Mandar catálogo". A successful
      Probarlo sends pass `done`.


      **Step 5:** "Enviale el link a tu socio" with `InvitePartner`. "Seguir" appears
      once a link is created and sends pass `done`.


      **Step 6:** `ExpenseCategoriesEditor`. "Seguir" sends pass `done`.


      The front decides nothing about progress.


      **Step tests:**

      - price-only mode, ending with one variety;

      - step 2 targets `stock_target`;

      - "Agregar otra variedad";

      - each pass is sent only after its owner''s save succeeds;

      - a failed save sends no pass and keeps what was typed.


      **End-to-end:**

      - **Full run**, from a phone viewport, in under 5 minutes: accept a new-business
      invitation, price a variety, load its opening stock, pass every optional step,
      reach "Listo", open Nueva venta and land on Inicio with no notice.

      - **Resume run:** leave after step 1 and resume from the notice into step 2.

      - **One fact, one place:** the step-3 value reads the same in Configuración.

      - **Storage:** browser storage holds only `tema`.


      **Design:** `compare.sh` for every Onboarding screen.'
    seams:
    - Onboarding step screens (Testing Library, fetch faked)
    - web/e2e onboarding runs
    depends_on:
    - shell
    - extract-catalog-message
    - extract-integrations
    - extract-users-and-categories
    - extract-variety-name
    - extract-stock-entry
    status: approved
    screens:
    - OnboardingVariedad
    - OnboardingStock
    - OnboardingMensaje
    - OnboardingBot
    - OnboardingSocio
    - OnboardingCategorias
    - OnboardingListo
    branch: agentic/GH-86.wiring

    reason: The OnboardingListo entry in screens.json now points at /onboarding/done
      with no steps. That path sends anyone without the listo flag to Inicio, so the
      entry compares Inicio against the Listo design and the Listo screen has still
      not been compared.
    return_category: standard-other
  integration:
    id: integration
    title: Integration
    brief: Every subtask branch is already on the task branch; make the whole thing
      work.
    conflicts: []
    seams: []
    depends_on: []
    status: approved

    reviewed_head: 455ddce7e7a888e881b3a3f8d90cf543fc4fb440
invariants:
- Code under app/onboarding/ never writes another capability's table and never imports
  another hexagon from domain/ or application/; each fact adapter lives in its owner's
  adapters/ and is wired only in app/main.py.
- Every Onboarding route takes the business only from CurrentBusinessId; the only
  request input is the pass body's step and as.
- No GET handler and no read use case in the onboarding hexagon issues an INSERT,
  UPDATE or DELETE.
- ended_at is set only by advance, record_pass or skip_rest (after re-reading both
  required facts in the same transaction) or by the import's OnboardingEnding, only
  while it is null, and nothing ever clears or moves it.
- Each onboarding use case that writes commits once, at its end, through the Transaction
  port; the import's ending writes under the import's NoCommit and never commits itself.
- 'progress() is total: resume is null only when ended_at is set, and otherwise names
  a step or finish.'
- The onboarding table carries a non-null business_id with uq_onboarding_business_id,
  follows §11 naming with no allowlist change, is in LABEL_AS_BUSINESS, and is never
  read by any has_records().
- No answer from /onboarding-progress carries an amount, grams, token, phone number
  or customer name; targets are {id, name} of a variety only.
- 'The front never draws "Listo" except right after a write answered over: true; a
  render or a mount never sends advance, a pass or skip-rest.'
- The front decides no done, passed, count, resume, target or over; it renders /onboarding-progress
  answers, and keeps nothing about the Onboarding in browser storage.
- An extract-* subtask edits no existing test file and changes no behaviour or look
  of the owner screen it touches.
- Every Onboarding step saves through its owner's existing route and shows the owner's
  refusal codes; no step restates a validation rule.

  location: web/src/screens/varieties/VarietyNew.tsx:1
  claim: The header comment still says VarietyNew does the POST /varieties and shows
    refusals under the name, but both now live in VarietyNameForm.
  severity: observation
  scope: subtask
  category: standard-other
  status: open
- subtask: integration
  version: 1
  location: app/varieties/adapters/onboarding_facts.py:3
  claim: The docstring says 'Varieties never imports the Onboarding', but the module
    implements an Onboarding port and imports from it.
  severity: observation
  scope: subtask
  category: standard-other
  status: open
settled_findings:
- web/src/screens/settings/IntegrationsForm.test.tsx:84
- web/src/screens/EntradaStock.tsx:154
- web/src/screens/settings/CatalogMessageForm.tsx:84
- web/src/screens/StockEntryForm.tsx:126
- web/src/screens/onboarding/VarietyStep.tsx:39
- web/src/screens/onboarding/stepBodies.tsx:24
- scripts/design/screens.json:84
- scripts/design/screens.json:78
approval_settled:
- subtask: extract-integrations
  location: web/src/screens/settings/IntegrationsForm.test.tsx:84
  claim: The test 'Buscar mi chat marks the form dirty' types '1' into the token before
    clicking Buscar mi chat, which already makes the form dirty, so it passes even
    if the filled own chat did not count as dirty and the brief's item has no effective
    test.
  version: 2
- subtask: extract-stock-entry
  location: web/src/screens/EntradaStock.tsx:154
  claim: 'The declared web-e2e check failed on the delivered head 5ef0ad1 and was
    not re-run. The brief requires the e2e stock run to pass as it was, and I reproduced
    one failure on the very screen this diff touches: after a click on ''+ Entrada
    de stock'', the entry screen never showed.'
  version: 2
- subtask: wiring
  location: web/src/screens/onboarding/stepBodies.tsx:24
  claim: compare.sh was not run for any of the seven Onboarding screens this subtask
    builds, so none of them has been checked by eye against its design, on phone or
    on computer.
  version: 3
- subtask: wiring
  location: scripts/design/screens.json:84
  claim: 'This diff points the OnboardingListo entry at /onboarding/done with no steps,
    but that path sends anyone without the {listo: true} history flag to Inicio, so
    the entry puts Inicio next to OnboardingListo.dc.html and the Listo screen has
    never been compared.'
  version: 3

design_items:
- role_slug: ux_lead
  kind: observation
  step: Main flow · Starts (Invitacion)
  text: The flow names Invitacion as the entry, but in the prototype Invitacion is
    the join screen ("Sumate a La Huerta"). The new-business acceptance is NegocioNuevo
    ("Creá el negocio"), and that screen's submit is what must land in the Onboarding.
- role_slug: ux_lead
  kind: observation
  step: Main flow · What can go wrong · import loads the business mid-flow
  text: The flow says the Onboarding "ends and shows 'Listo'". PM adjustment 1 settled
    that once an import loads the business, "Listo" is never shown and the after-import
    notice on Inicio takes over. The import also happens outside the Onboarding, so
    showing "Listo" there has no place in the walk. The flow must follow the PM's
    decision.
- role_slug: ux_lead
  kind: observation
  step: Step 1 · Onboarding step screen (refs VariedadNueva, PrecioNuevo) — sin prototipo
  text: 'In the prototype these are two separate screens and two saves: VariedadNueva
    takes only the name, and PrecioNuevo has list price, optional bulk price and minimum,
    with Rige desde fixed to Hoy. The flow''s "about 3 touches" does not say whether
    the step saves once or twice. It also does not say which refusal and discard dialog
    applies when the variety is saved but the price is not.'
- role_slug: ux_lead
  kind: observation
  step: Steps 1–2 · "Agregar otra variedad"
  text: The flow offers "Agregar otra variedad" after step 1 as well as after step
    2. That lets the seller create varieties without opening stock, and step 2 then
    has to pick one. The PRD defines the loop as name, price and opening stock per
    variety. The flow should offer the choice only after the stock and keep the loop
    as the PRD has it.
- role_slug: ux_lead
  kind: observation
  step: Step 2 · Onboarding step screen (ref EntradaStock) — sin prototipo
  text: 'EntradaStock has an Origen picker (Compra/Cosecha/Stock inicial/Ajuste) and
    a variety selector. Its "Mango Bomb" frame preselects Stock inicial and labels
    cost "Opcional para stock inicial". The flow says "picks a variety". It should
    say the step comes with the variety just created and Stock inicial already chosen.
    It also omits the line the PRD requires: with no cost, the lot costs $0.'
- role_slug: ux_lead
  kind: observation
  step: Step 4 · Telegram bot (ref ConfiguracionIntegraciones)
  text: The prototype needs a token, a channel chat (@usuario or link), and "Tu chat"
    ("Abrí tu chat con el bot y escribile cualquier cosa para verlo acá"), which sends
    the seller out to Telegram and back. Its test button reads "Enviarme una prueba",
    not "Probarlo". The flow's "2 touches" leaves out the token, the channel and the
    trip to Telegram. It should also say what is kept if the app reloads during that
    trip, since nothing is saved until the test succeeds.
- role_slug: ux_lead
  kind: observation
  step: Step 6 · Expense categories / Skip job
  text: The flow says step 6 has no "Saltar". The PRD's layout puts "Saltar" on every
    optional step (criterion 2). Neither dead-ends, because "Seguir" is also one tap,
    but the flow should state plainly which controls step 6 shows.
- role_slug: ux_lead
  kind: observation
  step: Main flow · Ends (passing step 6 with a required step undone)
  text: If a partner removes the only variety or stock entry while the seller is on
    steps 3–6, passing step 6 cannot end the Onboarding. The flow does not say where
    that tap lands. Presumably it is the resume step, step 1 or 2, and the flow should
    say so.
- role_slug: ux_lead
  kind: observation
  step: Leave and resume · Inicio notice "Seguí con la configuración" — sin prototipo
  text: Not drawn on Inicio or InicioCompu. InicioPrimeraVez's position 1 holds the
    "Para arrancar" checklist, and the notice takes that slot. The flow's order matches
    results.md, but its shape and wording were judged on the text alone.
- role_slug: ux_lead
  kind: observation
  step: '"Listo" and the Paso N de 6 progress indicator — sin prototipo'
  text: No screen in the prototype for the step layout, the progress indicator or
    "Listo". These were judged on the flow's text alone. Per the PRD, design must
    draw them from tokens before build.
- role_slug: ui_lead
  kind: observation
  step: 'Step container: "Paso N de 6"'
  text: 'The indicator is defined only as text. The PRD asks for progress in words
    and in shape. Draw the shape from tokens (for example segments in the blue accent
