## UX Lead: final walk

_`scripts/design/sample-app.sh` printed nothing in time:
(no output)_

_The system denied 2 write attempts: this role writes nothing; reproduce by reading the code, the task documents and the existing tests, or by running the declared checks. A denial here is not a syntax problem._

### Walk

**How this walk was done.** The sample app (`scripts/design/sample-app.sh`) printed nothing in time, so I never had an address or a user to sign in as. I also wasn't allowed to inspect the running processes or run the Playwright specs myself. So I could not open the app in a browser. Instead I walked each job through the built front:
- `web/src/screens/onboarding/*`;
- `session.ts`;
- `inicioNotices.ts`;
- the Onboarding end-to-end spec, `web/e2e/onboarding.spec.ts`, read but not run.

I compared all of it against the validated flow. The PM should take this as a reading of the code, not a walk of the running app, and may want the end-to-end runs confirmed green before closing.

**Job: set up a new business (main flow)**
- **Start.** In `session.ts`, a `new_business` accept lands on `/onboarding`, and a join accept lands on `/`. `/onboarding` resolves to the resume step. This matches the flow.
- **Step frame.** One column with no bar and no "+ Nueva venta". It has:
  - "Atrás" and "Cerrar";
  - "Paso N de 6" in words, plus the indicator's shape and an "Obligatorio"/"Opcional" badge;
  - the title and its one-line why;
  - on optional steps, "Saltar", "Saltar el resto" (only when `can_skip_rest`) and the line on where to do it later.

  One leave guard asks before "Atrás", "Cerrar" or "Saltar" discards typing. An untouched step leaves at once.
  - On step 1, "Atrás" is shown and goes to Inicio. The validated screens hid it there. Observation.
- **Step 1, a variety with today's price.** This takes two saves: the name ("Agregá la variedad"), then the price ("Guardá el precio"), with "Rige desde" fixed to today.
  - A refused price keeps the saved variety, and a retry sends only the price. This closes the earlier "one save or two" observation.
  - After the price, it goes straight to step 2 for that variety.
- **Step 2, opening stock.** The variety and Stock inicial are fixed, and the step shows "Si no cargás el costo, el lote cuesta $ 0.".
  - After saving, it offers "Seguir", which sends `advance`, or "Agregar otra variedad".
  - "Agregar otra variedad" is offered only after step 2, and the loop goes name, price, then that variety's stock. This closes the earlier loop observation.
- **"Atrás" from step 2 to step 1** after a variety is priced shows the empty name form, i.e. a new variety. It does not show the one already saved, which may surprise the seller. Observation.
- **Step 3, header and footer.**
  - Saving the message passes the step. The primary button keeps the owner's label, "Guardá el mensaje", not "Guardar".
  - "Dejar así" shows only while nothing is typed and passes the step.
  - "Saltar" works.
- **Step 4, Telegram bot.** This is the owner's form, plus the line "Cuando quieras, publicalo desde Variedades → Mandar catálogo".
  - A successful test passes the step and moves on.
  - A failed test shows its reason and passes nothing.
  - A bot saved earlier shows "Hecho" and "Seguir".
  - "Saltar" passes the step.
- **Step 5, a partner.** The title is "Enviale el link a tu socio" and the body is the owner's invite. "Seguir" shows once a link exists or a second user exists, and "Saltar" passes the step. It never waits for the partner to join.
- **Step 6, expense categories.** Edits save at once, and only "Seguir" passes the step as done.
  - "Saltar" is present and records the step as skipped. "Listo" then reads "Para después". This settles the earlier disagreement in favour of the PRD.
  - "Saltar el resto" still shows on step 6 once both required steps are done, though the consolidated screens said it would not. It does the same as "Saltar" there. Observation.
- **End.**
  - Only a write that answers `over: true` opens "Listo". A reload or deep link without that flag goes to Inicio.
  - If a required step was undone, the last pass lands on Inicio. The notice then reads "6 de 6 pasos" with no "Falta" line, and tapping it sends `advance`. This closes the earlier open question.
  - After an import, "Listo" is never shown, because the layout sends any Onboarding path of a business that is over back to Inicio.
- **"Listo".** One line per step:
  - "Hecho · N variedades" for step 1;
  - "Hecho · N entradas" for step 2, also a count;
  - "Hecho" for a passed optional step;
  - "Para después — en Configuración → …" for a skipped one.

  Its actions are "Cargar mi primera venta" and "Ir a Inicio", with no celebration.

**Job: leave and resume**
- "Cerrar" goes to Inicio and sends nothing.
- On Inicio, slot 1 shows "Seguí con la configuración" while the Onboarding is offered and not over. It reads "N de 6 pasos", shows the indicator and "Falta: <short name>", and links to the resume step. It cannot be dismissed.
- "Arrancá por las variedades" never shows with it. Slot 1 waits until both answers have arrived.
- The resume end-to-end run covers leaving after step 1 and coming back to "Paso 2 de 6".

**Job: skip optional steps**
- "Saltar" takes one tap, plus a discard confirmation if something is typed.
- "Saltar el resto" shows only once both required steps are done.
- Skipped steps are named in "Listo" with where to do them later.

**Job: first sale after setup**
- "Cargar mi primera venta" first swaps "Listo" for Inicio in the history, then opens Nueva venta. Closing or saving returns to Inicio, never to "Listo".
- The end-to-end run checks that no notice is left on Inicio afterwards.

**Unchanged jobs**
- A join accept still lands on Inicio, and the owner screens keep their chrome.

### Verdict

`approved`: every job in the validated flow can be finished in the built front, and nothing is a dead end. This was judged from the code and the end-to-end spec, because the sample app never printed an address and I could not open it or run the specs.

## UI Lead: final comparisons

### Comparisons

I ran `scripts/design/compare.sh` for the six steps, "Listo", step 1 in the dark theme, and Inicio, InicioOscuro and InicioCompu. Every screen rendered. I judged each one by eye, design next to the build.

- **Step frame (all steps):** matches the design: "Atrás" / "Cerrar", the "Paso N de 6" text with its Obligatorio/Opcional chip, the six-segment indicator (done filled, current outlined, so never colour alone), the title and the one-line reason.
  - *Observation:* "Saltar" ships as a full-width soft button and "Saltar el resto" as a separate link. The design has both as one line of text links.
- **OnboardingVariedad (step 1):** same chrome and title ("Cargá tu primera variedad"), and the same field styling.
  - *Observation:* the build shows the name alone ("Agregá la variedad"), then price on a second screen. The design has one form with name and price fields and "Guardá la variedad".
- **OnboardingStock (step 2):** the title, reason and variety ("Variedad: Mango Bomb") are there. The $0 line is there, and so is the "Seguir" / "Agregar otra variedad" state (in the code).
  - *Observation:* the full EntradaStock form is embedded: a date picker, a visible Variedad select, Nota, no "Stock inicial" chip and the button "Guardá la entrada".
  - *Observation:* the cost hint reads "Obligatorio cuando es una compra", while the $0 line sits under the button rather than under the cost.
- **OnboardingMensaje (step 3):** faithful, with the defaults, "Dejar así" and the skip line.
  - *Observation:* the primary button reads "Guardá el mensaje" where the design has "Guardar".
- **OnboardingBot (step 4):** has the token, the channel, Tu chat, "Probarlo", the publish line and the skip line.
  - *Observation:* "Buscar mi chat" is an inline link, not the soft button drawn.
  - *Observation:* the publish line shows before success.
- **OnboardingSocio (step 5):** has the title "Enviale el link a tu socio", the reason and the skip line.
  - *Observation:* the action is the soft "Invitá a un socio" instead of the primary "Crear el link".
- **OnboardingCategorias (step 6):** faithful list rows with Renombrar (blue) and Eliminar (amber), "+ Categoría" and "Seguir".
  - *Observation:* an extra always-visible "Nueva categoría" input.
  - *Observation:* the "se guarda al toque" line is not visible.
- **OnboardingListo:** practically identical: "6 de 6 pasos", the full indicator, "Listo", "Esto quedó armado en tu negocio.", the check and empty-circle rows with "Hecho · N variedades" / "Para después — en Configuración → …", "Cargar mi primera venta" and "Ir a Inicio". No celebration.
- **Inicio:** the notice ships in slot 1 as one tappable card: title, chevron, "2 de 6 pasos", the indicator, "Falta: mensaje del catálogo". There is no "Arrancá por las variedades" beside it, and the code covers the `finish` variant (no Falta line).
  - *Observation:* it sits above the period tabs; the design puts it below them.
- **Coverage gaps (observation):** the comparisons do not show:
  - the notice on InicioCompu or in the dark theme;
  - steps 2–6 and "Listo" in the dark theme;
  - the discard dialogs.

  The tokens used make these states likely right, but they were not seen.

Every screen and state the PRD requires shipped, and every built screen reads as the same design. None of the findings is blocking.

### Verdict

`approved`: all Onboarding steps, "Listo" and the Inicio notice shipped and match the design by eye. The differences are labels, button weights and the owner forms' extra fields, so all are observations.

## PM: final validation

### Verdict

`approved`: UX Lead and UI Lead both approved, and neither marked anything blocking. Every job in the validated flow can be finished in the built app, and every required screen and state matches the validated screens design by eye.

**About the UX walk:** UX Lead couldn't open the running app, because the sample app never printed an address. So the walk was done by reading the built front and `web/e2e/onboarding.spec.ts`, and the specs were not run. Before close, Tech Lead's own review should confirm that the Onboarding end-to-end runs (the full run and the resume run) pass.

**Observations for the pull request (none blocking):**
- **Step 1:** "Atrás" shows and goes to Inicio; the design hides it there. Going back to step 1 after a variety is priced shows an empty name form rather than the saved variety.
- **Step 6:** "Saltar el resto" still shows on step 6 and does the same as "Saltar".
- **Skip buttons:** "Saltar" is a full-width soft button and "Saltar el resto" a separate link. The design has both as one line of text links.
- **Step 1 save:** it saves in two parts (name, then price), where the design has one form. This settles the open question on a partial save.
- **Step 2:** it embeds the full EntradaStock form (date, a visible Variedad select, Nota, no "Stock inicial" chip). The cost hint reads "Obligatorio cuando es una compra", and the $0 line sits under the button.
- **Labels and button weights:**
  - step 3 says "Guardá el mensaje" instead of "Guardar";
  - step 4's "Buscar mi chat" is an inline link, and its publish line shows before the test succeeds;
  - step 5 uses the soft "Invitá a un socio" instead of a primary "Crear el link";
  - step 6 has an always-visible "Nueva categoría" input, and its "se guarda al toque" line is missing.
- **Inicio notice:** it sits above the period tabs; the design puts it below them.
- **Not compared:** the notice on InicioCompu and in the dark theme, steps 2–6 and "Listo" in the dark theme, and the discard dialogs.
